# Did You Know? — Phase III editorial audit ledger

27 November 2026. Live Supabase checkpoint: 2,401 unique references; 273 original archived rows preserved. Previous audit reported full chapter coverage (1,189/1,189). Re-query actual database on every run. This ledger records editorial work, not blanket certification or production rendering.

## Batch 10 (prior conversation checkpoint)

15 records source-reviewed and updated according to the previous report: Genesis 1:1, 1:2, 1:5, 2:4, 3:1, 8:11, 9:27; Exodus 2:3, 3:14, 17:7, 17:15, 19:5, 20:17, 29:9, 34:29. Their source notes reside in the live discovery table; confirm individual evidence if needed. No implication that other records are audited.

## Resume

Continue Genesis original-language entries excluding the 20 Genesis references listed above. Genesis 13:2, 13:7 and 16:13 were not completed in Batch 11. Finish the remaining Exodus original-language entries, then the remainder of the canon and categories. Keep per-reference source and verdict here; do not mistake link presence for full independent verification. Do not recreate filler. Historical evidence and illustration work has separate earlier checkpoints; re-audit as necessary. Production asset build previously passed; full quality checks and mirror deployment are not yet confirmed.
